This funding marks a 3X increase in valuation over Candid’s Series C fundraise in February 2025, which was led by Oak HC/FT.​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌​​﻿‌﻿‌‍​‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌‍​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‍‌‍​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‍‌‌‍​‍‌‍​‍‌‍​‍‌‍​﻿​﻿‍​‌‍‌‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‍‌‍​‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌​​﻿‌﻿‌‍​‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌‍​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‍‌‍​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‍‌‌‍​‍‌‍​‍‌‍​‍‌‍​﻿​﻿‍​‌‍‌‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‍‌‍​‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌

Healthcare providers have grown to unprecedented scale, and the legacy systems running billing cannot keep up with today’s complexity. Modern technology, including artificial intelligence, holds promise to finally rein in billing spend and lost collections. But layering AI on broken systems only creates new problems.​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‌​‌‍‌‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​﻿​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿​﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌​​﻿​‌​﻿‌​‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‌​‌‍‌‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​﻿​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿​﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌​​﻿​‌​﻿‌​‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌


Today, Candid Health serves as the financial system of record and core RCM platform for hundreds of leading U.S. healthcare providers across dozens of medical specialties, including some of the largest, most complex, and fastest growing provider organizations in the The Candid team deeply understands its customers' core challenges and has built a modern infrastructure to enable an agentic transformation of the healthcare revenue cycle,” said Alex Katz, Managing Director at Sixth Street Growth. “In addition to a differentiated technology offering and clear proof points applying agentic AI at scale in RCM, the thing that stood out to us was Candid’s obsessive focus on customer outcomes. We spoke with nearly 40 Candid customers. The feedback was consistently ‘off the charts’ and really emphasized how Candid's platform had transformed underlying practice operations.”​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‌​﻿‍​​﻿‍​‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍​‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​‌​﻿‌‌​﻿‌​​﻿‍​‌‍​‌‌‍​‍‌‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿​‍​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‌​﻿‍​​﻿‍​‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍​‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​‌​﻿‌‌​﻿‌​​﻿‍​‌‍​‌‌‍​‍‌‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌﻿​﻿​‍​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌


Candid Health is an AI-first revenue cycle management platform built for enterprise healthcare providers. The platform unifies clinical, billing, and provider data into a single model — giving its automation engine the full context it needs to work. A configurable rules engine and AI agents handle the work that billing teams used to do manually. Every remaining manual touch is tracked, so teams can continuously find and eliminate repetitive tasks.​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‍​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​﻿‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌‍‌​​﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍​‍​﻿‍‌​﻿​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‍​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌​​﻿‌﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‍​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​﻿‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌‍‌​​﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍​‍​﻿‍‌​﻿​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‍​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌​​﻿‌﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌

, Candid Co-founder and COO. “It affects individual patients and their families, the providers we serve and their organizations, as well as our society at large. Candid’s Series D follows surging demand by enterprise provider organizations across the U.S., combined with 190% year-over-year annual contracted run-rate revenue growth and 180% year-over-year net dollar retention in 2025.​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​﻿‌‍‌‍​﻿​﻿​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​﻿‍​​﻿‌​​﻿‍​‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌‌‌‍​‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍​‌​﻿​‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌​‌‍‌‍​﻿​‍​﻿​​​﻿​‌​﻿‌‍​﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​﻿​﻿‌‍‌‍​﻿​﻿​﻿‍​​﻿‌‍​﻿‍​​﻿‌​​﻿‍​‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌‌‌‍​‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍​‌​﻿​‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍​﻿​‍​﻿‌​‌‍‌‍​﻿​‍​﻿​​​﻿​‌​﻿‌‍​﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌

Candid Health is on a mission to rebuild the financial infrastructure of American healthcare, allowing providers to focus on delivering quality care. Trusted by more than 200 leading healthcare organizations, Candid’s RCM automation and AI platform leverages advanced automation to decrease the cost to collect and increase net collection rates. Sixth Street Growth provides growth equity and bespoke capital solutions to mid- and late-stage technology companies. Sixth Street Growth is the dedicated growth investing platform of Sixth Street, a leading global investment firm with over $135 billion in assets under management and committed capital. **Contacts​​​​‌﻿‍﻿​‍​‍‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌﻿​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌​‍﻿‍‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿​‍​‍​‍﻿​​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍‌‍​‍​‍​﻿‍‍​‍​‍‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​﻿‍‍​‍﻿﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍‍​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍﻿﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌​​‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍​﻿‍﻿‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​﻿‍﻿‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​﻿‍﻿‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿​​​﻿​‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿​​​﻿‌﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‌​﻿‍​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿‍‌​﻿​﻿​﻿‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​﻿﻿﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‌‌‌‌‌‌‌﻿​‍‌‍﻿​​﻿﻿‌‌‍‍​‌﻿‌​‌﻿‌​‌﻿​​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍‌​‌‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍​﻿‌‍​‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‍​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​​‌​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍‍‌‌‍‌​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‌​﻿​​‌‍‌​​﻿​‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​‍﻿‌​﻿​​​﻿‍‌​﻿‌​​﻿‌​​‍﻿‌​﻿‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​‍﻿‌​﻿‍‌​﻿‍‌‌‍​﻿​﻿‍‌​‍﻿‌‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍​‌​﻿​‌​﻿‍‌‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍​‍‌‍​‌‌‍​‌​﻿‌﻿​‍‌‍‌﻿‌​‌﻿‍‌‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍﻿﻿‌‍‌﻿‌‌​​‌‍﻿﻿‌﻿​﻿‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍​‌‌﻿‌​‌‍‍​​﻿﻿‌‌‍​﻿‌‍﻿﻿‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿​​​﻿​‌‌‍‌​‌‍‌​‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿​​​﻿‌﻿​﻿​﻿​﻿​‌​﻿‍​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌‍​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‍‌‌‍﻿​‌‍‌​‌﻿​‍‌‍‌‌‌‍﻿‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​​‍‌‌﻿﻿‌‍‍﻿‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍‌​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​﻿‌​‌​​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌‍​﻿​​​﻿‌﻿​﻿‌‌‌‍​﻿‌‍‌‍​﻿​‍‌‍‌​​﻿‍​​﻿‍‌​﻿​﻿​﻿‌‍​‍‌‌​﻿​‍​﻿​‍​‍‌‌​﻿‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿‍‌﻿‌​‌‍‌‌‌﻿‍​‌﻿‌​​‍‌‍‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌﻿​‍‌﻿​﻿‌﻿​​‌‍‌‌‌‍​﻿‌﻿‌​‌‍‍‌‌﻿‌‍‌‍‌‌​﻿﻿‌‌﻿​​‌﻿‌‌‌‍​‍‌‍﻿​‌‍‍‌‌﻿​﻿‌‍‍​‌‍‌‌‌‍‌​​‍​‍‌﻿﻿‌**
